Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the quarks structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 350. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 229 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to quarks structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 350/229 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on quarks structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 10. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 73 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 10 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the quarks structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 73. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
